class TCGEN05_LDST_INST_NAME<string Op, string shape, int lg2Count, bit packOrUnpack> {
  string name = "TCGEN05_" # Op                 # "_" # shape                 # "_x" # !shl(1, lg2Count)                 # !if(!eq(packOrUnpack, 1), !if(!eq(Op, "LD"), "_PACK", "_UNPACK"), "");
}
class TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO<int Veclen> {
  list<NVPTXRegClass> regs = !listsplat(B32, Veclen);
  list<string> reg_names = !foreach(x, !range(0, Veclen), "r" # x);
  string regstring = "{{" #                       !interleave(                         !foreach(n, !range(0, Veclen), "$r" # n), ", ") #                      "}}";
  dag Ins = !dag(ins, regs, reg_names);
  dag Outs = !dag(outs, regs, reg_names);
}
class TCGEN05_LD_INST<string Shape, int Num, bit Pack> :         NVPTXInst<(outs), (ins), "?", []> {
  TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO Info = TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO<                                 NVVM_TCGEN05_LDST_ACCESS_SIZE<Shape, Num>.veclen>;
  let InOperandList = !con((ins B32:$taddr),                            !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"),                                   (ins i64imm:$offset), (ins)));
  let OutOperandList = Info.Outs;
  let AsmString = "tcgen05.ld.sync.aligned"                   # "." # Shape                   # ".x" # !shl(1, Num)                   # !if(!eq(Pack, 1), ".pack::16b", "")                   # ".b32 "                   # Info.regstring # ", "                   # "[$taddr]"                   # !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"), ", $offset", "")                   # ";";
}
class TCGEN05_ST_INST<string Shape, int Num, bit Unpack> :         NVPTXInst<(outs), (ins), "?", []> {
  TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO Info = TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO<                                 NVVM_TCGEN05_LDST_ACCESS_SIZE<Shape, Num>.veclen>;
  let InOperandList = !con((ins B32:$taddr),                            !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"),                                   (ins i64imm:$offset), (ins)),                            Info.Ins);
  let OutOperandList = (outs);
  let AsmString = "tcgen05.st.sync.aligned"                   # "." # Shape                   # ".x" # !shl(1, Num)                   # !if(!eq(Unpack, 1), ".unpack::16b", "")                   # ".b32 [$taddr]"                   # !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"), ", $offset", "")                   # ", " # Info.regstring                   # ";";
}
let isConvergent = true, Predicates = [hasTcgen05InstSupport] in {
foreach shape = ["16x64b", "16x128b", "16x256b", "32x32b", "16x32bx2"] in {
  foreach num = !range(0, 8) in {
    foreach packOrUnpack = [false, true] in {
      if NVVM_TCGEN05_LDST_ACCESS_SIZE<shape, num>.valid then {
        def TCGEN05_LDST_INST_NAME<"LD", shape, num, packOrUnpack>.name :               TCGEN05_LD_INST<shape, num, packOrUnpack>;
        def TCGEN05_LDST_INST_NAME<"ST", shape, num, packOrUnpack>.name :               TCGEN05_ST_INST<shape, num, packOrUnpack>;
      }
    }
  }
}
}
class Tcgen05LdRedTypeProfile<int Num, bit hasOffset, ValueType RedValTy>:         SDTypeProfile<0, 0, []> {
  list<ValueType> Results = !listsplat(RedValTy, !add(Num, 1));
  list<ValueType> VTs = !listconcat(     Results,     [i32],     !if(!eq(hasOffset, 1), [i64], []),     [i32],     !if(!eq(RedValTy, f32), [i1, i1], [])   );
  let Constraints = !foreach(x, !range(!size(VTs)), SDTCisVT<x, VTs[x]>);
  let NumResults = !size(Results);
  let NumOperands = !sub(!size(VTs), NumResults);
}
class Tcgen05LdRedSDNode<string Shape, int Num, string Type>:         SDNode<"NVPTXISD::TCGEN05_LD_RED_" # Shape # "_X" # Num # "_" # !toupper(Type),         Tcgen05LdRedTypeProfile<Num, !eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"), !cast<ValueType>(Type)>,                     [SDNPHasChain, SDNPSideEffect, SDNPMemOperand]>;
class Tcgen05LdRedInst<string Shape, int Num, string RedOp, string Type,                        bit Abs = 0, bit Nan = 0>:          NVPTXInst<(outs), (ins), "?", []>,          Requires<[hasTcgen05LdRedSupport]> {
  bit IsFloat = !eq(Type, "f32");
  string TypeStr = !if(IsFloat, "f32", "u32");
  SDNode Opcode = Tcgen05LdRedSDNode<Shape, Num, Type>;
  TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO Info = TCGEN05_LDST_REGINFO<Num>;
  let InOperandList = !con((ins B32:$addr),                            !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"), (ins i64imm:$offset),                                                        (ins)));
  let OutOperandList = !con(Info.Outs, (outs B32:$redVal));
  string AbsStr = !if(!and(!eq(IsFloat, 1), !eq(Abs, 1)), ".abs", "");
  string NanStr = !if(!and(!eq(IsFloat, 1), !eq(Nan, 1)), ".NaN", "");
  let AsmString = "tcgen05.ld.red.sync.aligned"                   # "." # Shape                   # ".x" # Num                   # "." # RedOp                   # AbsStr # NanStr                   # "." # TypeStr # " "                   # Info.regstring # ", $redVal, [$addr]"                   # !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"), ", $offset", "")                   # ";";
  int RedOpVal = !cond(     !eq(RedOp, "min") : 0,     !eq(RedOp, "max") : 1,   );
  int AbsVal = !if(!eq(Abs, 1), -1, 0);
  int NanVal = !if(!eq(Nan, 1), -1, 0);
  dag IntrinsicPattern = !con((Opcode i32:$addr),                               !if(!eq(Shape, "16x32bx2"), (Opcode i64:$offset),                                                           (Opcode)));
  dag FlagOperands = !con((Opcode (i32 RedOpVal)),                           !if(!eq(IsFloat, 1), (Opcode (i1 AbsVal), (i1 NanVal)),                                                (Opcode)));
  dag Results = !con(!foreach(tmp, OutOperandList,                                    !subst(outs, set,                                    !subst(B32, !if(!eq(IsFloat, 1), f32, i32), tmp))),                      (set 0));
  let Pattern = [!setdagarg(Results, !sub(!size(Results), 1),                                      !con(IntrinsicPattern, FlagOperands))];
}
let isConvergent = true in {
  foreach shape = ["32x32b", "16x32bx2"] in {
    foreach num = 1...8 in {
      foreach redop = ["min", "max"] in {
        defvar access_size = NVVM_TCGEN05_LDST_ACCESS_SIZE<shape, num>;
        if access_size.valid then {
          foreach abs = [0, 1] in {
            foreach nan = [0, 1] in {
                def : Tcgen05LdRedInst<shape, access_size.veclen, redop, "f32", abs, nan>;
              }
            }
          def : Tcgen05LdRedInst<shape, access_size.veclen, redop, "i32">;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

class Tcgen05MMABase<bit IsSparse, string ASpace, string Kind, int CtaGroup,                      string CollectorUsage, string CollectorUsageB = "discard"> :         NVPTXInst<(outs), (ins), "?", []>,         Requires<[]> {
  int CollectorUsageVal = !cond(     !eq(CollectorUsage, "discard"): 0,     !eq(CollectorUsage, "lastuse"): 1,     !eq(CollectorUsage, "fill"): 2,     !eq(CollectorUsage, "use"): 3   );
  int CollectorUsageAVal = CollectorUsageVal;
  int KindVal = !cond(                   !eq(Kind, "f16"): 0,                   !eq(Kind, "tf32"): 1,                   !eq(Kind, "f8f6f4"): 2,                   !eq(Kind, "i8"): 3,                   !eq(Kind, "ti16"): 4,                 );
  bit HasCollectorBQualifier = !ne(CollectorUsageB, "discard");
  int CollectorUsageBVal = !cond(     !eq(CollectorUsageB, "discard"): 0,     !eq(CollectorUsageB, "lastuse"): 1,     !eq(CollectorUsageB, "fill"): 2,     !eq(CollectorUsageB, "use"): 3   );
  dag SparseMetadataIns = !if(IsSparse, (ins B32:$spmetadata), (ins));
  string SparseMetadataStr = !if(IsSparse, ", [$spmetadata]", "");
  dag SparseMetadataIntr = !if(IsSparse, (ins i32:$spmetadata), (ins));
  string AOperandStr = !if(!eq(ASpace, "tensor"), "[$a]", "$a");
  NVPTXRegClass ARegClass = !if(!eq(ASpace, "tensor"), B32, B64);
  dag BasePatternArgs = !con((ins i32:$dtmem, ARegClass:$a, i64:$b,                                  i32:$idesc, i1:$enable_inp_d),                              SparseMetadataIntr);
  dag BaseInOperandList = !con((ins B32:$dtmem, ARegClass:$a, B64:$b,                                     B32:$idesc, B1:$enable_inp_d),                                SparseMetadataIns);
  let OutOperandList = (outs);
  string Prefix = "tcgen05.mma";
  string SpCtaKindStr = !if(IsSparse, ".sp", "")                         # ".cta_group::" # CtaGroup                         # ".kind::" # Kind;
  string BaseOperandsCommonStr = " [$dtmem], "                                  # AOperandStr                                  # ", $b";
  string IDescStr = ", $idesc";
  string BaseOperandsStr = BaseOperandsCommonStr # SparseMetadataStr # IDescStr;
  string InputDStr = ", $enable_inp_d";
}
class Tcgen05MMAInst<bit IsSparse, string ASpace, string Kind, int CtaGroup,                      string CollectorUsageA, string CollectorUsageB,                      bit IsScaleInputD, bit IsAShift> :         Tcgen05MMABase<IsSparse, ASpace, Kind, CtaGroup, CollectorUsageA,                        CollectorUsageB> {
  let Predicates = !listconcat(     !if(HasCollectorBQualifier, [hasRubinFamilySupport], []),     !cond(       !eq(Kind, "ti16") : [hasRubinFamilySupport],       IsScaleInputD : [hasTcgen05MMAScaleInputDImm],       !eq(Kind, "i8") : [hasTcgen05MMAI8Kind],       true : [hasTcgen05InstSupport]     )   );
  Intrinsic Intrin = !cast<Intrinsic>(                         NVVM_TCGEN05_MMA<IsSparse, ASpace, IsAShift, IsScaleInputD>.record_name                      );
  dag ScaleInpIns = !if(IsScaleInputD, (ins i64imm:$scale_input_d), (ins));
  string ScaleInpStr = !if(IsScaleInputD, ", $scale_input_d", "");
  dag ScaleInpIntr = !if(IsScaleInputD, (Intrin i64:$scale_input_d), (Intrin));
  let InOperandList = !con(BaseInOperandList, ScaleInpIns);
  let AsmString = Prefix                   # SpCtaKindStr                   # !if(IsAShift, ".ashift", "")                   # ".collector::a::" # CollectorUsageA                   # !if(HasCollectorBQualifier, ".collector::b::" # CollectorUsageB, "")                   # BaseOperandsStr                   # InputDStr                   # ScaleInpStr                   # ";";
  dag IntrinsicPattern = !con(!foreach(tmp, BasePatternArgs, !subst(ins, Intrin, tmp)),                               ScaleInpIntr);
  dag FlagOperands = (Intrin (i32 KindVal), (i32 CtaGroup),                              (i32 CollectorUsageAVal), (i32 CollectorUsageBVal));
  let Pattern = [!con(IntrinsicPattern, FlagOperands)];
}
foreach sparse = [0, 1] in {
  foreach space = ["tensor", "shared"] in {
    foreach kind = ["f16", "tf32", "f8f6f4", "i8", "ti16"] in {
      foreach cta_group = [1, 2] in {
        foreach collector_usage_a = ["discard", "lastuse", "fill", "use"] in {
          foreach scale_input_d = !if(!or(!eq(kind, "f16"),                                           !eq(kind, "tf32")), [0, 1], [0]) in {
            foreach ashift = !if(!eq(space, "tensor"), [0, 1], [0]) in {
              foreach collector_usage_b = ["discard", "lastuse", "fill", "use"] in {
                def : Tcgen05MMAInst<sparse, space, kind, cta_group,                                     collector_usage_a, collector_usage_b,                                     scale_input_d, ashift>;
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
class Tcgen05MMADisableOutputLaneTypeProfile<bit IsSparse, string ASpace,                                              int CtaGroup, bit IsScaleInputD>:         SDTypeProfile<0, 0, []> {
  int DisableOutputLaneVecSize = !mul(4, CtaGroup);
  list<ValueType> VTs = !listconcat(     [i32],     !if(!eq(ASpace, "tensor"), [i32], [i64]),     [i64, i32, i1],     !if(IsSparse, [i32], []),     !if(IsScaleInputD, [i64], []),     !listsplat(i32, DisableOutputLaneVecSize),     [i32],     [i32],     [i32]   );
  let Constraints = !foreach(x, !range(!size(VTs)), SDTCisVT<x, VTs[x]>);
  let NumOperands = !size(Constraints);
}

  string DisableOutputLaneStr = "{{" #                                   !interleave(                                     !foreach(x,                                       !range(DisableOutputLaneVecSize),                                               "$disable_output_lane" # x),                                     ", ")                                 # "}}";
  dag InOperandList = !con(BaseInOperandList,                            ScaleInpIns,                            DisableOutputLaneIns);
  let AsmString = Prefix                   # SpCtaKindStr                   # !if(IsAShift, ".ashift", "")                   # ".collector::a::" # CollectorUsageA                   # !if(HasCollectorBQualifier, ".collector::b::" # CollectorUsageB, "")                   # BaseOperandsStr                   # ", " # DisableOutputLaneStr                   # InputDStr                   # ScaleInpStr                   # ";";
  dag IntrinsicPattern = !con(!foreach(tmp, BasePatternArgs, !subst(ins, Opcode, tmp)),                               ScaleInpIntr,                               DisableOutputLaneInput);
  dag FlagOperands = (Opcode (i32 KindVal), (i32 CollectorUsageAVal),                              (i32 CollectorUsageBVal));
  let Pattern = [!con(IntrinsicPattern, FlagOperands)];
}

    }
  }
  def TENSORMAP_REPLACE_TILE_ELEMTYPE_ # ss :     TensormapReplaceInst_2<ss_ptx, "elemtype", "b32", B32, i32,       int_nvvm_tensormap_replace_elemtype, as>;
  def TENSORMAP_REPLACE_SWIZZLE_ATOMICITY_ # ss :     TensormapReplaceInst_2<ss_ptx, "swizzle_atomicity", "b32", B32, i32,       int_nvvm_tensormap_replace_swizzle_atomicity, as>,     Requires<[hasTensormapReplaceSwizzleAtomicitySupport]>;
  def TENSORMAP_REPLACE_SWIZZLE_MODE_ # ss :     TensormapReplaceInst_2<ss_ptx, "swizzle_mode", "b32", B32, i32,       int_nvvm_tensormap_replace_swizzle_mode, as>;
}
class NVPTXReg<string n> : Register<n> {
  let Namespace = "NVPTX";
}
class NVPTXRegClass<list<ValueType> regTypes, int alignment, dag regList>      : RegisterClass <"NVPTX", regTypes, alignment, regList>;
def VRFrame32         : NVPTXReg<"%SP">;
def VRFrame64         : NVPTXReg<"%SP">;
def VRFrameLocal32    : NVPTXReg<"%SPL">;
def VRFrameLocal64    : NVPTXReg<"%SPL">;
def VRDepot  : NVPTXReg<"%Depot">;
foreach i = 0...4 in {
  def P#i  : NVPTXReg<"%p"#i>;
  def RS#i : NVPTXReg<"%rs"#i>;
  def R#i  : NVPTXReg<"%r"#i>;
  def RL#i : NVPTXReg<"%rd"#i>;
  def RQ#i : NVPTXReg<"%rq"#i>;
}
foreach i = 0...31 in {
  def ENVREG#i : NVPTXReg<"%envreg"#i>;
}
def B1 : NVPTXRegClass<[i1], 8, (add (sequence "P%u", 0, 4))>;
def B16 : NVPTXRegClass<[i16, f16, bf16], 16, (add (sequence "RS%u", 0, 4))>;
def B32 : NVPTXRegClass<[i32, v2f16, v2bf16, v2i16, v4i8, f32], 32,                               (add (sequence "R%u", 0, 4),                               VRFrame32, VRFrameLocal32)>;
def B64 : NVPTXRegClass<[i64, v2i32, v2f32, f64], 64,                         (add (sequence "RL%u", 0, 4),                          VRFrame64, VRFrameLocal64)>;
def B128 : NVPTXRegClass<[i128], 128, (add (sequence "RQ%u", 0, 4))>;
def SpecialRegs : NVPTXRegClass<[i32], 32, (add VRFrame32, VRFrameLocal32, VRDepot,                                             (sequence "ENVREG%u", 0, 31))>;
